# Prior review status (2026-09-29 findings, as of a2b55b3)

Every 2026-09-29 finding id mentioned by any intent card (and the F-ids cited by `local://audit-leads.md`), with the 2026-09-29 ledger and finding-index status next to the status observed at a2b55b3. Sources are `intent/<crate>.md` sections; `safety-invariants.md` and `contracts.md` are cited where they add evidence. Neither file contradicts a card on any finding status.

**Drift column:** "Index drift" = `finding-index.md` disagrees with `implementation-ledger.json`. "Ledger drift" = the cards show code evidence that neither file records.

## Drift summary

- **Index drift (8):** G01, G07, G14, G17, G18, G19 (index Open, ledger verified); G15, T01 (index Open, ledger partial). The cards agree with the ledger in every case; contracts.md R11 independently reports the G07 index drift.
- **Ledger drift (6):** CS05, CS06, CS07 (ADR 0036 / e96798d / e1a1771 work not recorded), T04 (a5c4cdb fix landed, ledger partial), G10 and F05 (invalidation landed, ledger open).
- No card found a ledger "verified" finding that has regressed.
- Not in the ledger at all but ranked among the ten worst gaps by `safety-invariants.md` §6: gaps 1 (no runtime wrong-gravity defense), 2 (inert danger zone), 6 (reference error exits without cleanup), 7 (HoldTracking latch untested), 8 (ZeroSta/AddOffset writable), 9 (trace I/O in tick) and 10 (MIT envelope untested). They are leads L-armee-dynamics-12, L-davout-26, L-davout-27, L-berthier-18, L-robstride-17, L-berthier-07 and L-davout-30.
